## POST /v1/attendance-periods/{period_id}/finalize
<a id="post-v1-attendance-periods-period-id-finalize"></a>

**Finalize Attendance Period**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance-periods/{period_id}/finalize` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.LOCK` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |

| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | no textual reference found |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:1590`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L1590) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_period_legal_entity.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Closes and locks the period — `AttendanceDay.is_locked=true` across its whole range,
a frozen `AttendancePeriodSummary` snapshot, and refuses fail-closed
(`ATTENDANCE_FINALIZATION_BLOCKED`) unless a fresh validation is `READY`.

| `period_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |

| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

Fail-closed finalization-readiness check (architecture §5) — always re-derives READY/
BLOCKED live; never returns a stale cached result. `POST`, not `GET`, since it also records
a `VALIDATED`/`VALIDATION_FAILED` action and refreshes the period's cached validation
status (audit-visible, per Phase 10's "validation failure" requirement).

| Status | Code | Meaning / trigger | Recommended client behaviour | Safe to retry |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHORIZED / INVALID_TOKEN` | Missing, malformed, expired or revoked access token, inactive user, ended session, or invalid company membership. | Refresh once (`POST /v1/auth/token/refresh`), replay; if refresh fails go to sign-in. | yes (after refresh) |
| 403 | `PERMISSION_DENIED` | The caller's roles do not grant the required permission (`ATTENDANCE.POLICY_MANAGE`). | Hide/disable the action; do not retry. | no |
| 403 | `COMPANY_PENDING_APPROVAL / COMPANY_SUSPENDED` | Company is read-only; writes refused (also COMPANY_REJECTED / COMPANY_TERMINATED for any request). | Show read-only banner; do not retry. | no |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Also returned (with `Resource not found`) when `X-Company-Id` is sent and differs from the token's company. | Omit the header or send the token's `company_id`. | no |
| 422 | `REQUEST_VALIDATION_ERROR / INVALID_IF_MATCH` | `If-Match` header missing, or not an integer version (`3`, `"3"`, `W/"3"`). | Send the `version` from the last read. | yes (after fix) |
| 409 | `STALE_RESOURCE (see module error list)` | Version in `If-Match` no longer matches the stored row. | Re-fetch, re-apply the user's edit, resubmit with the new version. | after re-fetch |
| 422 | `REQUEST_VALIDATION_ERROR` | Body/query/path failed schema validation; `details[]` has `field` (dotted location) + `code` + `message`. | Map `details[].field` onto form fields. | yes (after fix) |
| 503 | `DATABASE_BUSY` | Lock/statement timeout, deadlock or serialization failure (transaction rolled back). `Retry-After: 2`. | Retry with backoff. | yes |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unhandled server error. Body carries `request_id` for support. | Show generic error + `request_id`. For writes, re-read state before retrying (outcome of a lost response is unknown). | reads: yes; writes: only if idempotent |

## GET /v1/attendance-policy-resolution
<a id="get-v1-attendance-policy-resolution"></a>

**Get Policy Resolution**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `GET /v1/attendance-policy-resolution` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.POLICY_MANAGE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **B** - Manager / HR workflow (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/policy_repository.dart`, `features/attendance/presentation/policy_resolution_screen.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:769`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L769) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_policy_resolution.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Admin preview of the WD-09 precedence walk for one employee+date+policy_kind — mirrors
`/calendar-resolution` (W1). `policy_kind` must be one of `ATTENDANCE_POLICY`/
`OVERTIME_POLICY`/`REGULARIZATION_POLICY`; anything else 422s via the service's fail-closed
resolution (never guesses).

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `employee_id` | query | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `date` | query | yes | string(date) | - | - |
| `policy_kind` | query | yes | string | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X GET "$BASE_URL/v1/attendance-policy-resolution?employee_id=<employee_id>&date=<date>&policy_kind=<policy_kind>" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN"
```

## POST /v1/attendance/days/calculate
<a id="post-v1-attendance-days-calculate"></a>

**Calculate Attendance Day**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/days/calculate` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.VIEW_ALL` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **B** - Manager / HR workflow (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:955`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L955) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_concurrency.py`, `test_attendance_day.py`, `test_attendance_edge_cases.py` (+14) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

On-demand calculation for one employee+date — creates the row if none exists yet, or
fully replaces its derived fields if it does (WD-10). COMPANY-scoped
(`ATTENDANCE.VIEW_ALL`) rather than `ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE`: this is the ordinary "compute
today's attendance" path, not the privileged backdated-recompute act — see
`POST /attendance/days/{id}/recompute` for that.

`date` is now optional (`ED-2`, execution gate §9.3) — omitting it defaults server-side to
`business_today(company_tz)`, so the client no longer has to compute "today" itself (the
web client's own `todayIso()`, a UTC calendar date, was `NEW-02`). Passing an explicit date
is unaffected and still honoured exactly as before.

| `employee_id` | query | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `date` | query | no | string(date) | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/attendance/days/calculate?employee_id=<employee_id>" \
```

## POST /v1/attendance/days/calculate-range
<a id="post-v1-attendance-days-calculate-range"></a>

**Calculate Attendance Range**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/days/calculate-range` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 202 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart`, `features/attendance/presentation/attendance_period_detail_screen.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:1008`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L1008) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_range_calculate.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

PAY-001 — queues calculation of every employed day in the range (at most 62 days, not
past today) so a period can be finalized without one call per employee-date. Privileged
(`ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE`, COMPANY). Locked days are skipped. Progress is visible through the
period's `validate` verdict.

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request body (`application/json`, required)

Schema: [AttendanceRangeCalculateInput](../models/attendance.md#attendancerangecalculateinput)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `date_from` | yes | string(date) | no | - | - |
| `date_to` | yes | string(date) | no | - | - |
| `employee_ids` | no | array&lt;string(uuid)&gt; | yes | minItems=1, maxItems=1000 | - |
| `legal_entity_id` | no | string(uuid) | yes | - | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/attendance/days/calculate-range" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"date_from":"2026-01-15","date_to":"2026-01-15","employee_ids":["0a1b2c3d-4e5f-4a6b-8c7d-9e0f1a2b3c4d"],"legal_entity_id":"7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7"}'
```

```json
  "legal_entity_id": "7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7"
}
```

## POST /v1/attendance/days/{attendance_day_id}/recompute
<a id="post-v1-attendance-days-attendance-day-id-recompute"></a>

**Recompute Attendance Day**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/days/{attendance_day_id}/recompute` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart`, `features/attendance/data/policy_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:985`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L985) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_day.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Privileged recompute (`ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE`, COMPANY) — architecture §18: backdated
policy publishes or other after-the-fact corrections re-resolve versions by date and fully
replace the day's derived fields. Refused on a locked day (`AttendanceDayLockedError`,
409) — nothing in W4 can lock a day yet, so this path is exercised by tests only, ready for
W8's `AttendanceLock`.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `attendance_day_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

## POST /v1/attendance/days/{attendance_day_id}/work-on-leave/keep-leave
<a id="post-v1-attendance-days-attendance-day-id-work-on-leave-keep-leave"></a>

**Keep Leave Over Punches**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/days/{attendance_day_id}/work-on-leave/keep-leave` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:1030`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L1030) |
| Automated tests | path-only: `test_leave_projection.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

BUS-012 — HR confirms an approved full-day leave stands over punches on the same day
(same privileged `ATTENDANCE.RECOMPUTE` authority as recompute). Clears the
`WORK_ON_LEAVE_UNRESOLVED` finalize blocker for this day. 409 if the day is locked or has no
conflict to resolve.

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `attendance_day_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | yes | string | no | minLength=1, maxLength=1000 | - |

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/attendance/days/<attendance_day_id>/work-on-leave/keep-leave" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"Example reason"}'
```

every other angle must match it; the result waits for HR approval before it can punch.

## POST /v1/attendance/face-punches/me
<a id="post-v1-attendance-face-punches-me"></a>

**Create Face Punch**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/face-punches/me` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.PUNCH` |
| Rate limit | face_punch: setting `face_punch_requests_per_minute_per_ip` per tenant+IP per 60s |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 201 |
| Mobile suitability | **A** - Yes - employee self-service (data-scope limited; on-behalf variants need COMPANY scope) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:2005`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L2005) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_face_liveness_session.py`, `test_face_punch.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Step 2 of a face punch. Same contract as `POST /attendance/punches/me` — no time, source
or employee field; the server stamps the instant — plus `liveness_session_id` (from step 1)
and exactly `frames_required` JPEG/PNG `frames` captured after it was issued. The server runs
its own liveness model on every frame, then matches the best live frame against the
caller's enrolled face; only then is a punch written. No client-side liveness result is
accepted. A request without a challenge (an outdated app sending `photo`) is refused.

`idempotency_key` (optional, 16-64 of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`): resend the same key when retrying
after a lost response (with a fresh challenge and capture) and the original result is
returned instead of a second punch. The same key with a different direction is a 409.

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request body (`multipart/form-data`, required)

Schema: [Body_create_face_punch_v1_attendance_face_punches_me_post](../models/shared.md#body_create_face_punch_v1_attendance_face_punches_me_post)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `direction` | yes | enum `IN`/`OUT` | no | - | - |
| `frames` | no | array&lt;string&gt; | yes | - | - |
| `liveness_session_id` | no | string(uuid) | yes | - | - |
| `idempotency_key` | no | string | yes | - | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/attendance/face-punches/me" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -F "direction=IN" \
  -F "frames=['Example frames']" \
  -F "liveness_session_id=3fa85f64-5717-4562-b3fc-2c963f66afa6" \
  -F "idempotency_key=Example idempotency key"
```

### Successful response

**201** - Successful Response

Envelope: `{ "data": { ... }, "meta": {"request_id", "server_time"} }` - `data` item is [FacePunchRead](../models/attendance.md#facepunchread)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `punch` | yes | [PunchRead](../models/attendance.md#punchread) | no | - | - |
| `similarity` | yes | decimal (JSON **string**) | no | - | - |
| `liveness_score` | no | decimal (JSON **string**) | yes | - | - |

```json
    "punch": {
      "id": "7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7",

  }
}
```

## POST /v1/attendance/punches
<a id="post-v1-attendance-punches"></a>

**Create Punch On Behalf**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/punches` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.PUNCH_ON_BEHALF` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 201 |
| Mobile suitability | **B** - Manager / HR workflow (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:836`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L836) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_day.py`, `test_attendance_edge_cases.py`, `test_attendance_period.py` (+5) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

HR/admin entering a punch for an employee (`ATTENDANCE.PUNCH_ON_BEHALF`, COMPANY).

This route previously served both acts and picked the required permission from
`body.employee_id` — meaning FastAPI had to parse and trust a caller-controlled field before
it could decide what the caller was allowed to do. Splitting the routes (`ED-4`) makes the
requirement static: a caller holding only `ATTENDANCE.PUNCH` now gets a clean 403 here,
regardless of what they put in the body.

A caller naming *their own* `employee_id` here is accepted rather than redirected — they
trivially hold the scope, and the resulting row is legitimately an `ADMIN`-source correction.

`scope`/`caller_employee_id` still gate the *target* employee inside the service: holding the
permission is not the same as being allowed to see this particular person.

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request body (`application/json`, required)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `employee_id` | yes | string(uuid) | no | - | - |
| `punch_time` | yes | string(date-time) | no | - | - |
| `direction` | yes | string | no | - | - |
| `reference` | yes | string | no | minLength=1, maxLength=200 | - |
| `idempotency_key` | no | string | yes | maxLength=100 | - |

## POST /v1/attendance/punches/me
<a id="post-v1-attendance-punches-me"></a>

**Create Self Punch**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/attendance/punches/me` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.PUNCH` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 201 |
| Mobile suitability | **A** - Yes - employee self-service (data-scope limited; on-behalf variants need COMPANY scope) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/attendance_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:795`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L795) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_attendance_security.py`, `test_employee_default_role_attendance.py`, `test_face_base_release.py` (+2) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Employee self-service punch (`ATTENDANCE.PUNCH`, SELF).

**The permission is static** — `Depends(require_permission(...))` resolves it before the body
is read, so no value a caller controls can route them into a different authorization branch.
That is the security half of `ED-4`; the schema half is that `PunchCreateSelf` has no
`punch_time`, `source`, or `employee_id` field to send.

The punch instant is assigned by the server at receipt (`requested_punch_time=None`).

| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

| `reference` | no | string | yes | maxLength=200 | - |
| `idempotency_key` | no | string | yes | maxLength=100 | - |

## POST /v1/leave-projections/{projection_id}/cancel
<a id="post-v1-leave-projections-projection-id-cancel"></a>

**Cancel Leave Projection**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/leave-projections/{projection_id}/cancel` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ATTENDANCE.LEAVE_PROJECTION_SYNC` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | no textual reference found |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:1713`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L1713) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_leave_projection.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Withdraws an approved projection (`status=CANCELLED`, never a delete — provenance is
preserved) and synchronously recomputes the affected `AttendanceDay`. Refused with
`ATTENDANCE_PERIOD_CLOSED` if the date's period is locked (architecture §21 — "default
refuse"; the only correction path for a locked date is a full period reopen, PD-LOCK-2).

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `projection_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `If-Match` | header | yes | string | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request body (`application/json`, required)

Schema: [LeaveProjectionCancelInput](../models/attendance.md#leaveprojectioncancelinput)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | yes | string | no | minLength=1, maxLength=1000 | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/leave-projections/<projection_id>/cancel" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "If-Match: <resource version>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"Example reason"}'
```

matches the caller's own; on-behalf (`ATTENDANCE.REGULARIZATION_REQUEST_ON_BEHALF`, COMPANY)
when it names someone else — mirrors `create_punch`'s direct `rbac.resolve_data_scope()`
call exactly, since the gating permission here also depends on the request body.

§14: `MANAGER` stage requires the caller to *be* the snapshotted

## POST /v1/regularizations/{request_id}/cancel
<a id="post-v1-regularizations-request-id-cancel"></a>

**Cancel Regularization Request**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/regularizations/{request_id}/cancel` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | none at route level (+ resolved inside handler: `ATTENDANCE.REGULARIZATION_REQUEST`, `ATTENDANCE.REGULARIZATION_REQUEST_ON_BEHALF`) |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **A** - Yes - employee self-service (data-scope limited; on-behalf variants need COMPANY scope) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | no textual reference found |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py:1313`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/attendance/router.py#L1313) |
| Automated tests | path-only: `test_regularization.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Only the original submitter may cancel (enforced in the service); this route accepts
either submission permission since a cancel is available to whichever kind of submitter
created the request — the service's `submitted_by_user_id` check is the real gate, this is
only "does the caller hold some regularization-submission grant at all".

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |

| `request_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
